Algorithm detail:
Input a0 length n, normalize.
For each iteration:
  c=conv(a)
  exact = max c; score=2n exact. update best.
  choose top K indices. If K=1, same as max subgradient top1.
  weights = exp((c[top]-cmax)*beta) / sum.
  gradient g = 2 * gradient of weighted sum. If K small direct loop:
      g zeros n
      for idx,k in enumerate(top):
        w=weights[idx]*2
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        j=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
        g[lo:hi+1] += w*a[k-j]
    This derivative is for p not normalized? Since p simplex, objective φ(p) (with sum=1) derivative as above. Multiplicative natural gradient: log p_new = log p - η g (maybe scale). Actually natural gradient projected onto simplex is p * exp(-η g)/sum. We should scale g to unit norm to set η as KL step. If g has zeros? For top k, g nonzero.
  step: p = p * exp(-η * g / scale?) If g entries are O(max a*weights). For n=2000, a~0.0005, g~1e-3. If η=1, exp small. Scale g by max or L2 to tune.
Use adaptive learning rate: start 5? Actually p_i ~5e-4; g_i ~2*w*a_j ~1e-3. η* g ~5e-3, fine. But if some a_j high 0.01, g~0.02, η=5 ->0.1. Could use normalize g by its mean absolute or max.
Natural gradient step may be too small. We can use momentum: m = μ m + g_scaled. Update p *= exp(-η m). Normalize.
Adaptive: if exact score improves, keep/increase η; if no improve, decrease. Since smooth φ maybe not monotonic. Could track φ (weighted sum) or exact.
No-improvement restart: add Gaussian noise in log p.

But top-K with K large and β moderate may optimize smooth upper bound, not exact. We can anneal β from low (e.g., 100) to high (e.g., 1e5) to balance. Since c differences ~1e-4; β=1000 gives weights sensitive to 1e-4*1000=.1. β=10000 highlights top. Start low to explore, increase.
Maybe use temperature like: weights over all 2n-1 with softmax β. If β low, gradient uses many constraints -> flattened smoothing. For c~5e-4, β=1e4, top differences .001-0.01? Actually top c max .00076, second .00070 diff 6e-5*1e4=.6. So top few. β=1e6 gives exp(-10) for diff 1e-5. Use all weights? Could compute gradient via FFT if all weights. But top K=50 with softmax over top approximates.

Could use "mirror descent with adaptive step and line search on exact max". Potential issue: Multiplicative gradient using derivative of unnormalized objective? Since p sum=1, the derivative of φ with constraint sum=1 should be projected: g_proj = g - mean(g) perhaps. Multiplicative normalization effectively subtracts some? p*exp(-ηg)/sum; for small η, log p_new = log p - η(g - mean(g) + η/2(var - mean^2)...). So it does subtract mean implicitly. Good.

Another optimizer: use projected gradient with Barzilai-Borwein step on simplex for additive. Could improve local. Let p simplex. Gradient g (real). Take p_new = project_simplex(p - step*g). Project onto simplex can be done O(n log n) via sort, expensive. Multiplicative avoids.

Low-dimensional global search:
- Use continuous parameterization with exp polynomial of degree d and maybe phase. Use scipy.optimize.minimize on log objective? Could find good basins. Then full refiner.

Let's write gradient topK:
```
def _topk_gradient(a, c, K, temp):
    n=len(a)
    m=int(min(K, len(c)))
    top = np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]
    top_c = c[top]
    cm = top_c.max()
    if temp <= 0: temp=1e-300
    e=np.exp((top_c-cm)/temp)
    e/=e.sum()
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx,k in enumerate(top):
        w=2.0 * e[idx]
        if w ==0: continue
        k=int(k)
        lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1, k)
        if lo>hi: continue
        # g[lo:hi+1] += w * a[k - np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
        # create np.arange maybe costly; can precompute index arrays? For each k varies.
        j = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
        g[lo:hi+1] += w * a[j]
    return g, float(c.max()), float(cm)
```
If K=1, same. To speed up, avoid `np.arange` each loop by using slice reversal: a[j] where j = k-lo down to k-hi? Actually j = k - i for i=lo..hi, so j from k-lo down to k-hi. Could use `a[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]` but end calc. For integer slices, `a[k-lo: max(k-hi, -1) : -1]`. Let's keep arange maybe okay. K=32, n=2000 -> 64k elements per iter, arange overhead okay. Could precompute all? no.
Maybe use `segments = np.empty`? We'll keep simple.

Need c = conv(a). `_fft_convolve(a)` with power2. We can precompute size? Helper.

Optimizer loop pseudo:
```
def _optimize_sm(a0, time_budget, rng, K, temp_frac_start, temp_frac_end, eta, mu, restart_patience=200):
    n=len(a0); a=a0.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=... return; a/=s
    L=2*n-1; fft_size=_next_pow2(L)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=max(conv(a)); best_score=2n*best_c
    eta_current=eta; mu_current=mu; temp_frac=temp_frac_start
    last та旭日 time
    for it in range(max_iters):
       c=_fft_convolve(a, fft_size)
       cur=c.max()
       score=2n*cur
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: update; no_improve=0; maybe if long print
       else no_improve++
       # temp anneal by time or iters
       if it % 50==0: temp_frac = start * (end/start)**(it/anneal_iters)?
       temp = max(1e-12, cur*temp_frac)
       g, topmax = _topk_gradient(a,c,n,K,temp)
       gmax = g.max()
       if gmax <= 0: break
       m = mu*m + (1-mu)*(g/gmax)
       scale = m.max()
       if scale<=0: break
       a = a * np.exp(-eta_current * m / scale)  # m/scale in [?,1]
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300); a/=a.sum()
       # adapt eta

           eta_current *= 0.8; if eta_current < small -> restart
       elif no_improve > patience//2:
           temp_frac = max(temp_frac_end, temp_frac*0.9)
       elif no_improve < patience//4:
           eta_current=min(eta_current*1.05, eta_max)

So implement:
```
def _grad_conv_weights(a, w, fft_size):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)  # w real length <=2n-1 padded
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), fft_size)
    return 2.0 * corr[:n]
```
Since rfft with conj real? `np.conj(fa)`. Need fw and fa same size. Good. This computes gradient for arbitrary real weights w_k = ∇(∑ w b_k) /2? Actually ∂∑ w_k b_k /∂a_i = 2∑ w_k a_{k-i}. So h=corr; grad=2*h.

Pseudo optimizer more concrete:
```
def _optimize_smooth(a0, budget, rng, temp_start=0.3, temp_end=0.005, eta=0.2, mu=0.75, patience=3000, label=''):
    n=len(a0); a=np.maximum(a0,0.0).astype(np.float64)
    s=a.sum(); if s<=1e-15: return a0, np.inf
    a /= s
    fft_size=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    m=np.zeros(n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=np.inf; best_smooth=np.inf
    no_improve_smooth=0; no_improve_exact=0
    step=eta
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+budget
    last_print=start
    prev_score=None
    it=0
    velocity? no.
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fft_size)
        c=c[:2*n-1]
        c=np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        # optionally renormalize c sum? Not needed.
        cur=float(c.max())
        score=2.0*n*cur
        if score < best_score:
            best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve_exact=0
        else:
            no_improve_exact +=1
        # smooth
        # if cur == 0: break
        z=(c-cur)/temp
        exp_z=np.exp(z)
        s_exp=float(exp_z.sum())
        if s_exp <=0 or not np.isfinite(s_exp):
            w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
        else:
            w=exp_z/s_exp
        smooth = cur + temp * (np.log(s_exp) - np.log(2*n-1))
        if smooth < best_smooth:
            best_smooth=smooth; no_improve_smooth=0
        else:
            no_improve_smooth +=1
        # grad
        if np.any(w):
            fw=np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
            g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa), fft_size)[:n]
            g=np.maximum(g,0.0)? Should be nonnegative but numerical.
            gmax=float(g.max())
        else: break
        if not finite or gmax<=0: break
        gs=g/gmax
        momentum = mu*m + (1-mu)*gs
        mmax=float(momentum.max())
        if mmax<=0: break
        a = a * np.exp(-step * momentum / mmax)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        a /= a.sum()
        # adapt step
        if prev_score is not None:
            if score > prev_score * 1.05:  # this score is old a? Wait prev_score from previous old a? We update after; not good.
```

Let's craft final code with top-level functions:
- `_next_pow2(n)`
- `_convolve_with_fft(a)`: returns fa,c. Could take fft_size for efficiency.
```
def _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, ff_size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fft_size)
    m=2*a.size-1
    c=c[:m]
    c[c<0]=0
    return fa, c
```

   # At top, fa,c correspond to current a.
   cur = float(c.max()); score=2n*cur
   if score < best_score:
      best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_im_exact=0
      print every 10s maybe
   else: no_im_exact +=1

   if not finite/s<=0: handle

      no_im +=1
   # If no_im too large, adjust/restart

       temp_frac = min(0.8, max(temp_frac*1.5, temp_start*0.7))

       momentum *= 0.5

           continue
       else:

Do `it +=1; continue` to avoid update with same gradient after temp change? But that wastes. Instead set temp_frac and recompute weights now? Simpler: after no_im large, perform a "reheat": reset temp_frac and do not update? We'll `continue` after updating it. Let's implement restart more directly: if no_im > patience, perturb and recompute; continue.
   if no_im > patience//2:
       temp_frac = max(temp_end, min(0.8, temp_frac * 0.98))? Hmm.

   if it > 0 and anneal_iters>0:

       temp_frac = max(target, temp_end)

   # gradient
   fw = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)

   mmax = float(momentum.max())
   if mmax <=0: break

   a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)

   elif new_score < old_score * (1.0 - 1e-7):

   elif no_im > 500:
       eta_current *= 0.97
   if not finite or new_score > 1e100: fallback
   it += 1
   if it % 2000 ==0 and time-last_print>10: print(label it score)

def _quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf
    a=a/s
    fft_size=_next_pow2(2*len(a)-1)

    return 2.0*len(a)*float(c.max())
```

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(n): ...

def _normalize_positive(a, min_val=1e-300):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a

def _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size):
    m = 2*len(a)-1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, fft_size)
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return fa, c

def _quick_score(a):
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf
    n=len(a); fft_size=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    _, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c))
```
`nan_to_num` with posinf? values not inf. okay.

`_fft_corr_weights(a, w, fa, fft_size)` maybe separate:
```
def _gradient_from_weights(w, fa, fft_size, n):
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
    g=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa), fft_size)
    g=g[:n]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)
```

`_optimize_smooth_mult` as above.
Let's write it carefully.
```
def _optimize_smooth_mult(a0, time_budget, rng, temp_start=0.30, temp_end=0.005, eta=0.15, mu=0.70, patience=6000, anneal_frac=0.6, min_eta=0.01, max_eta=0.6, label=''):
    a = _normalize_positive(a0)
    if a is None or time_budget <= 0:
        return None, np.inf
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
        return a, np.inf
    fft_size = _next_pow2(2*n-1)
    m = 2*n-1
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + time_budget
    fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    score = 2.0*n*cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    anneal_time = max(30.0, min(900.0, time_budget*anneal_frac))
    anneal_start = start
    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta_cur = eta
    no_im = 0
    no_im_exact=0
    restarts=0
    it=0
    last_print=start
    # initial smooth? not necessary
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cur = float(np.max(c))
        if cur <= 1e-300:
            break
        score = 2.0*n*cur
        if score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_im_exact = 0
        else:
            no_im_exact += 1
        # anneal temp
        frac = (time.monotonic()-anneal_start)/anneal_time
        if frac > 1.0: frac=1.0
        if temp_start > temp_end:
            temp_frac = temp_start * (temp_end/temp_start) ** frac
        else:
            temp_frac = temp_start
        temp = max(1e-300, cur * temp_frac)
        z = (c - cur) / temp
        exp_z = np.exp(z)
        s_exp = float(np.sum(exp_z))
        if not np.isfinite(s_exp) or s_exp <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = exp_z / s_exp
        log_mean = float(np.log(s_exp) - np.log(m))
        smooth = cur + temp * log_mean
        # no_im smooth track: We need best_smooth variable.
```
Need initialize `best_smooth`. At top before loop, compute initial smooth? We can set `best_smooth = score` and then if smooth< best. But smooth with temp may differ. Initialize:

```

```
        if smooth < best_smooth - 1e-12:
            best_smooth=smooth; no_im=0
        else:
            no_im += 1
        if no_im > patience:
            # restart
            noise = 0.05 + 0.03*(restarts % 4)
            a = best_a.copy()
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)) + 1e-7
            a = _normalize_positive(a)
            if a is None: a=best_a.copy()
            fa,c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
            cur=float(np.max(c)); score=2*n*cur
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
            momentum.fill(0.0)
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, max(min_eta, eta*1.1))
            anneal_start = time.monotonic()
            no_im=0; no_im_exact=0; restarts +=1; it=0
            continue
        g = _gradient_from_weights(w, fa, fft_size, n)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        gs = g / gmax
        momentum = mu * momentum + (1.0 - mu) * gs
        mmax = float(np.max(momentum))
        if not np.isfinite(mmax) or mmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        step_scale = eta_cur
        if step_scale <= 0: break
        a = a * np.exp(-step_scale * momentum / mmax)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        a_sum = float(np.sum(a))
        if a_sum <= 1e-300:
            a = best_a.copy()
            eta_cur *= 0.5
            continue
        a = a / a_sum
        old_score = score
        fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
        new_score = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
        if new_score < best_score:
            best_score = new_score; best_a = a.copy()
        # adapt eta
        if new_score > old_score * 1.03:
            eta_cur *= 0.7
        elif new_score < old_score * (1.0 - 1e-8):
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, eta_cur * 1.03)
        elif no_im > 2000:
            eta_cur *= 0.97
        elif no_im < 300:
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, eta_cur * 1.005)
        if eta_cur < min_eta:
            eta_cur = min_eta
        it += 1
        if it % 5000 == 0:
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now-last_print > 10.0:
                print(f"  [{label}] it={it} score={best_score:.6f} cur={cur:.3e} temp_frac={temp_frac:.4f} eta={eta_cur:.4f}")
                last_print=now
    return best_a, best_score

def _run_optimizer(a0, time_alloc, rng, cfg, label, best, best_score):
    if a0 is None or time_alloc < 20:
        return best,best_score
    a_opt, sc = _optimize_smooth_mult(a0, time_alloc, rng, **cfg)
    if a_opt is not None:
        sc2 = _quick_score(a_opt)
        if not np.isfinite(sc): sc2=sc
        if sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2; best = a_opt.copy()
        # exact evaluate if reasonable
        n=len(a_opt)
        if n <= 7000:
            try:
                ev = evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist())
                if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_score:
                    best_score=ev; best=a_opt.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
    return best,best_score

Need `_resample_seq` top-level.
```
def _resample_seq(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size <=1 or n<2: return None
    if a.size == n: return a.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size,endpoint=False)
    xn = np.arange(n, dtype=float)/float(n)
    y=np.interp(xn, xs, a)
    return y
```
Maybe if a has endpoint high at last index, endpoint=False approximates x=1-1/n. Fine.

Gradient function:
```
def _gradient_from_weights(w, fa, fft_size, n):
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
    g = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), fft_size)
    g = g[:n]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


"""
Searches for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2)

Strategy:
- Use FFT-based convolution and an FFT-based gradient for a smoothed
  log-sum-exp surrogate of the maximum convolution coefficient.
- Normalize sequences to sum 1 and optimize in probability space with
  multiplicative natural-gradient updates (implicitly projects onto the
  non-negative simplex by renormalization).
- Anneal the softmax temperature from broad balancing to near-exact max
  chasing, with momentum, adaptive step size, and/log-noise restarts.
- Try multiple starts: the provided sequence, resamplings, randomized
  Fourier/beta/power shapes, and several optimizer configurations.
"""


def _next_pow2(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size):
    m = 2 * len(a) - 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, fft_size)
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return fa, c


def _gradient_from_weights(w, fa, fft_size, n):
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, fft_size)
    g = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), fft_size)
    g = g[:n]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def _quick_score(a):
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None or a.size < 2 or a.size > 20000:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    fft_size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    _, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _resample_seq(a, n):
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None or n < 2:
        return None
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    y = np.interp(xn, xs, a)
    return _normalize_positive(y)


def _add_candidate(cands, name, a):
    if len(cands) > 500:
        return
    a = _normalize_positive(a)
    if a is None:
        return
    s = _quick_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        cands.append((s, name, a))


def _generate_candidates(rng, best_a, max_n=6000, max_candidates=20):
    cands = []
    cid = 0

    if best_a is not None:
        a = _normalize_positive(best_a)
        if a is not None:
            cid += 1
            _add_candidate(cands, f"best_{cid}", a)

            cid += 1
            _add_candidate(cands, f"best_rev_{cid}", a[::-1].copy())

            for noise in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
                cid += 1
                b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, a.size))
                b = b + 1e-7
                _add_candidate(cands, f"best_noise_{noise}_{cid}", b)

            for ns in (1500, 2048, 2500, 3000, 4096, 5500):
                if 2 <= ns <= max_n:
                    cid += 1
                    b = _resample_seq(a, ns)
                    if b is not None:
                        _add_candidate(cands, f"resample_{ns}_{cid}", b)

    n_list = []
    for ns in (2048, 3000, 4096):
        if 2 <= ns <= max_n:
            n_list.append(ns)
    if not n_list and max_n >= 2000:
        n_list.append(2000)

    for n in n_list:
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

        # Tuned sine/cosine-like asymmetric starts.
        for amp in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7):
            for phase in (np.pi / 6.0, np.pi / 4.0, np.pi / 3.0, 2.0 * np.pi / 3.0):
                cid += 1
                a = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
                _add_candidate(cands, f"cos_{n}_{amp}_{phase:.2f}_{cid}", a)

                cid += 1
                a = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
                _add_candidate(cands, f"sin_{n}_{amp}_{phase:.2f}_{cid}", a)

        # Beta-like power shapes: p controls left endpoint, q right endpoint.
        for p in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
            for q in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
                if p + q <= -0.99:
                    continue
                cid += 1
                logv = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
                np.log(x + 1e-6, out=logv)
                logv *= p
                tmp = 1.0 - x + 1e-6
                logv += q * np.log(tmp)
                a = np.exp(np.clip(logv, -50.0, 50.0))
                _add_candidate(cands, f"beta_{n}_{p}_{q}_{cid}", a)

        # Random low-order Fourier starts.
        for _ in range(10):
            cid += 1
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            M = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
            for m in range(1, M + 1):
                amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.65))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.01)
            _add_candidate(cands, f"randcos_{n}_{cid}", a)

            cid += 1
            b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            M = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            for m in range(1, M + 1):
                amp = float(rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                b *= np.exp(0.5 * amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph))
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.01)
            _add_candidate(cands, f"randexp_{n}_{cid}", b)

    cands.sort()
    return cands[:max_candidates]


def _optimize_smooth_mult(a0, time_budget, rng,
                          temp_start=0.30, temp_end=0.005,
                          eta=0.15, mu=0.70,
                          patience=6000, anneal_frac=0.6,
                          min_eta=0.01, max_eta=0.60,
                          l2=0.0, label="opt"):
    a = _normalize_positive(a0)
    if a is None or time_budget <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return a, np.inf

    fft_size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    conv_len = 2 * n - 1
    log_conv_len = np.log(float(conv_len))

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + time_budget

    fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    score = 2.0 * n * cur

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_smooth = np.inf

    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eta_cur = eta
    no_im = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = start

    anneal_time = max(30.0, min(900.0, time_budget * float(anneal_frac)))
    anneal_start = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cur = float(np.max(c))
        if cur <= 1e-300:
            break

        score = 2.0 * n * cur
        if score < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        frac = (time.monotonic() - anneal_start) / anneal_time
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0

        if temp_start > temp_end:
            temp_frac = temp_start * (temp_end / temp_start) ** frac
        else:
            temp_frac = temp_start
        temp = max(1e-300, cur * temp_frac)

        z = (c - cur) / temp
        exp_z = np.exp(z)
        s_exp = float(np.sum(exp_z))

        if not np.isfinite(s_exp) or s_exp <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(conv_len, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = exp_z / s_exp

        if l2 > 0.0:
            c_sum = float(np.sum(c))
            if c_sum > 0.0:
                w = w + l2 * (c / c_sum)
                w_sum = float(np.sum(w))
                if w_sum > 0.0:
                    w = w / w_sum

        log_mean = float(np.log(s_exp) - log_conv_len)
        smooth = cur + temp * log_mean
        if smooth < best_smooth - 1e-14:
            best_smooth = smooth
            no_im = 0
        else:
            no_im += 1

        if no_im > patience:
            noise = 0.05 + 0.03 * (restarts % 4)
            a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)) + 1e-7
            a = _normalize_positive(a)
            if a is None:
                a = best_a.copy()

            fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            score = 2.0 * n * cur
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()

            momentum.fill(0.0)
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, max(min_eta, eta * 1.1))
            anneal_start = time.monotonic()
            no_im = 0
            restarts += 1
            it = 0
            continue

        g = _gradient_from_weights(w, fa, fft_size, n)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        gs = g / gmax
        momentum = mu * momentum + (1.0 - mu) * gs
        mmax = float(np.max(momentum))
        if not np.isfinite(mmax) or mmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        a = a * np.exp(-eta_cur * momentum / mmax)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        a_sum = float(np.sum(a))

        if a_sum <= 1e-300:
            a = best_a.copy()
            fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
            score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
            eta_cur = max(min_eta, eta_cur * 0.5)
            continue

        a = a / a_sum
        old_score = score

        fa, c = _convolve_with_fft(a, fft_size)
        new_score = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
        if new_score < best_score:
            best_score = new_score
            best_a = a.copy()

        if new_score > old_score * 1.03:
            eta_cur *= 0.70
        elif new_score < old_score * (1.0 - 1e-9):
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, eta_cur * 1.03)
        elif no_im > 2000:
            eta_cur = max(min_eta, eta_cur * 0.97)
        elif no_im < 300:
            eta_cur = min(max_eta, eta_cur * 1.005)

        if not np.isfinite(eta_cur) or eta_cur <= 0.0:
            eta_cur = min_eta

        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()
        if it % 5000 == 0 and now - last_print > 10.0:
            print(f"    [{label}] it={it} best={best_score:.7f} cur_score={score:.7f} "
                  f"temp_frac={temp_frac:.5f} eta={eta_cur:.5f}")
            last_print = now

    return best_a, best_score


def _run_optimizer(a0, time_alloc, rng, cfg, label, best, best_score):
    if a0 is None or time_alloc <= 25.0:
        return best, best_score

    a_opt, sc_opt = _optimize_smooth_mult(a0, max(0.0, time_alloc - 2.0), rng, label=label, **cfg)
    if a_opt is None:
        return best, best_score

    if np.isfinite(sc_opt):
        sc = sc_opt
    else:
        sc = _quick_score(a_opt)

    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best = a_opt.copy()
        print(f"  [{label}] internal best improved to {best_score:.7f} (n={a_opt.size})")

    # Use the official evaluator sparingly to guard against FFT roundoff / edge cases.
    if a_opt.size <= 8000:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence(a_opt.tolist())
            if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_score:
                best_score = ev
                best = a_opt.copy()
                print(f"  [{label}] official best improved to {best_score:.7f} (n={a_opt.size})")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=None, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-evaluation non-negative sequence within the time budget."""
    start = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s is None:
        limit = 2330.0
    else:
        try:
            limit = float(budget_s)
        except Exception:
            limit = 2330.0

    limit = min(2330.0, max(limit, 120.0))
    deadline = start + limit - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    provided = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if arr.size >= 2 and s > 0.01:
            provided = arr / s
    except Exception:
        provided = None

    if provided is not None:
        best = provided.copy()
        best_score = _quick_score(best)
        max_n = 6400 if limit > 1200 else 4096
    else:
        init_cands = _generate_candidates(rng, None, max_n=3000, max_candidates=8)
        if init_cands:
            best = init_cands[0][2].copy()
            best_score = init_cands[0][0]
        else:
            best = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64)
            best_score = 2.0
        max_n = 4096

    print(f"Initial best: score={best_score:.7f}, n={best.size}")

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        candidates = _generate_candidates(rng, best, max_n=max_n, max_candidates=18)
    else:
        candidates = []

    cfg_broad = {
        "temp_start": 0.35, "temp_end": 0.008,
        "eta": 0.18, "mu": 0.65,
        "patience": 8000, "anneal_frac": 0.65,
        "min_eta": 0.02, "max_eta": 0.55,
        "l2": 0.02
    }
    cfg_mid = {
        "temp_start": 0.18, "temp_end": 0.004,
        "eta": 0.12, "mu": 0.75,
        "patience": 6000, "anneal_frac": 0.60,
        "min_eta": 0.015, "max_eta": 0.45,
        "l2": 0.05
    }
    cfg_narrow = {
        "temp_start": 0.07, "temp_end": 0.0015,
        "eta": 0.07, "mu": 0.65,
        "patience": 4500, "anneal_frac": 0.55,
        "min_eta": 0.01, "max_eta": 0.35,
        "l2": 0.0
    }
    cfg_very = {
        "temp_start": 0.03, "temp_end": 0.0008,
        "eta": 0.04, "mu": 0.60,
        "patience": 3000, "anneal_frac": 0.50,
        "min_eta": 0.006, "max_eta": 0.25,
        "l2": 0.0
    }
    cfg_slow = {
        "temp_start": 0.50, "temp_end": 0.010,
        "eta": 0.06, "mu": 0.85,
        "patience": 10000, "anneal_frac": 0.70,
        "min_eta": 0.01, "max_eta": 0.35,
        "l2": 0.03
    }

    # Main basin: focused iterative refinement of the best known start.
    t = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.30 * limit)
    if t > 30.0:
        print(f"\nTask main_broad, alloc={t:.0f}s")
        best, best_score = _run_optimizer(best, t, rng, cfg_broad, "main_broad", best, best_score)

    t = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.16 * limit)
    if t > 25.0:
        print(f"\nTask main_mid, alloc={t:.0f}s, current best={best_score:.7f}")
        best, best_score = _run_optimizer(best, t, rng, cfg_mid, "main_mid", best, best_score)

    t = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.10 * limit)
    if t > 25.0:
        print(f"\nTask main_narrow, alloc={t:.0f}s, current best={best_score:.7f}")
        best, best_score = _run_optimizer(best, t, rng, cfg_narrow, "main_narrow", best, best_score)

    # Explore alternative basins from generated starts.
    explore_cfgs = [cfg_slow, cfg_broad, cfg_mid, cfg_narrow, cfg_mid]
    for i in range(min(5, len(candidates))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a0 = candidates[i][2]
        t = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.07 * limit)
        if t > 25.0:
            cfg = explore_cfgs[i % len(explore_cfgs)]
            print(f"\nTask cand_{i}, init={candidates[i][0]:.7f}, n={a0.size}, alloc={t:.0f}s")
            best, best_score = _run_optimizer(a0, t, rng, cfg, f"cand_{i}", best, best_score)

    # Try larger discretizations of the current best shape.
    for ns in (2048, 3072, 4096, 5600):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        if ns == best.size:
            continue
        a_r = _resample_seq(best, ns)
        if a_r is None:
            continue
        t = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.06 * limit)
        if t > 25.0:
            print(f"\nTask resample_{ns}, current best={best_score:.7f}, alloc={t:.0f}s")
            best, best_score = _run_optimizer(a_r, t, rng, cfg_mid, f"resample_{ns}", best, best_score)

    # Final polishing.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        t = min(remaining, remaining * 0.55)
        print(f"\nTask final_narrow, alloc={t:.0f}s")
        best, best_score = _run_optimizer(best, t, rng, cfg_narrow, "final_narrow", best, best_score)

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30.0:
        t = min(remaining, remaining * 0.60)
        print(f"\nTask final_very, alloc={t:.0f}s")
        best, best_score = _run_optimizer(best, t, rng, cfg_very, "final_very", best, best_score)

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64)

    best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
    best = np.nan_to_num(best, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best))
    if best.size < 2 or s <= 0.01:
        best = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(best))
    best = best / s

    final_internal = _quick_score(best)
    final_official = np.inf
    if best.size <= 8000:
        try:
            final_official = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
        except Exception:
            final_official = final_internal

    print(f"\nFinal: internal={final_internal:.7f}, official={final_official:.7f}, "
          f"n={best.size}, elapsed={time.monotonic() - start:.1f}s")

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
